## Overview

This roundup covers 296 Oracle security vulnerabilities. CVSS base scores range from 7.1 to 9.8. None are reported as actively exploited at the time of release. The issues affect APEX, Application Development Framework, Application Testing Suite, AutoVue, Autonomous Health Framework, Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition, Communications Billing and Revenue Management, E-Business Suite, E-Business Suite Integrated SOA Gateway, Fusion Middleware, Hyperion Financial Close Management, Hyperion Financial Data Quality Management, Enterprise Edition, Hyperion Financial Management, Hyperion Infrastructure Technology, Hyperion Planning, Java SE, Mobile Field Service, MySQL Server, Oracle Access Manager, Oracle Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition, Oracle Collaborative Planning, Oracle Database Server, Oracle E-Business Suite, Oracle E-Business Suite Integrated SOA Gateway, Oracle E-Business Suite Secure Enterprise Search, Oracle E-Business Suite iProcurement, Oracle Enterprise Manager Base Platform, Oracle Financial Services Customer Screening, Oracle Fusion Middleware, Oracle Hyperion Financial Close Management, Oracle Hyperion Infrastructure Technology, Oracle Hyperion Planning, Oracle Managed File Transfer, Oracle Mobile Field Service, Oracle REST Data Services, Oracle Service Bus, Oracle VM VirtualBox, Oracle WebCenter Portal, PeopleSoft Enterprise PeopleTools, Product Lifecycle Analytics, Service Delivery Platform, Siebel CRM, Siebel CRM Deployment, VM VirtualBox, WebCenter Portal, WebCenter Sites, WebLogic Server, WebLogic Server Proxy Plug-In, iProcurement.

## Recommendation

Review affected products and apply Oracle's November 2026 security updates.

## CVE-2026-101653

Oracle Mobile Field Service, a component of Oracle E-Business Suite, contains a vulnerability in its Internal Operations module. The flaw allows a low-privileged, network-adjacent attacker using HTTPS to achieve a full takeover of the application. The vulnerability carries a CVSS 3.1 base score of 8.8, indicating high impact on confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101653

## CVE-2026-101663

Oracle Mobile Field Service within Oracle E-Business Suite (versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15) contains a vulnerability in the Admin UI component. The vulnerability is remotely exploitable by an unauthenticated attacker via HTTP, potentially leading to unauthorized access, modification, or deletion of critical data.

Affected products:
- Oracle Mobile Field Service (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101663

## CVE-2026-127308

CVE-2026-127308 is a vulnerability in the Security component of Oracle AutoVue version 21.1.0. An unauthenticated attacker can leverage this network-accessible flaw via HTTP to gain unauthorized access to or modify critical data. The attack requires human interaction, and the vulnerability carries a CVSS 3.1 base score of 8.1.

Affected products:
- AutoVue (21.1.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-127308

## CVE-2026-91516

CVE-2026-91516 is a high-severity denial-of-service vulnerability in the Authentication Engine of Oracle Access Manager versions 12.2.1.4.0 and 14.1.2.1.0. An unauthenticated remote attacker can exploit this flaw over TCP to cause the application to hang or crash, resulting in a complete loss of availability. The vulnerability is network-accessible and does not require user interaction or high privileges.

Affected products:
- Oracle Access Manager (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.1.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-91516

## CVE-2026-127331

CVE-2026-127331 is a vulnerability in the Security component of Oracle AutoVue version 21.1.0. It allows an unauthenticated remote attacker to cause a denial-of-service (hang or crash) via HTTP network access. The vulnerability is characterized by a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.5, indicating a high impact on system availability.

Affected products:
- AutoVue (21.1.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-127331

## CVE-2026-114887

CVE-2026-114887 is an easily exploitable vulnerability in the Oracle Mobile Field Service component of Oracle E-Business Suite (versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15). The flaw allows a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP to perform unauthorized creation, deletion, modification, or access to critical data within the application. The vulnerability carries a CVSS 3.1 base score of 8.1, indicating high impacts on confidentiality and integrity.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)
- Oracle Mobile Field Service

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114887

## CVE-2026-91507

CVE-2026-91507 is a high-severity vulnerability in the Authentication Engine of Oracle Access Manager, allowing an unauthenticated, network-adjacent attacker to perform unauthorized data modification, deletion, or access. The vulnerability supports scope change, potentially impacting other integrated products, and carries a CVSS base score of 9.3.

Affected products:
- Oracle Access Manager (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.1.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-91507

## CVE-2026-101652

Oracle Mobile Field Service, a component of Oracle E-Business Suite versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15, contains a vulnerability in its Internal Operations module. An attacker with low privileges and network access via HTTP can exploit this vulnerability to perform unauthorized creation, deletion, or modification of critical data within the application, impacting both data confidentiality and integrity.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)
- Oracle Mobile Field Service

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101652

## CVE-2026-101659

Oracle E-Business Suite's Mobile Field Service component (Admin UI) contains a vulnerability affecting versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15. A low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP can achieve unauthorized creation, deletion, or modification of critical data. The vulnerability has a CVSS 3.1 score of 8.2 and allows for scope change, potentially impacting other integrated products.

Affected products:
- E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101659

## CVE-2026-101693

A vulnerability in the Security component of Oracle Hyperion Financial Data Quality Management, Enterprise Edition version 11.2.26.0.000 allows a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP to potentially take over the application. The vulnerability is characterized by a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.5, indicating high impact on confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

Affected products:
- Hyperion Financial Data Quality Management, Enterprise Edition (11.2.26.0.000)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101693

## CVE-2026-101848

Oracle Hyperion Financial Data Quality Management, Enterprise Edition version 11.2.26.0.000 contains a security vulnerability that allows an unauthenticated, network-based attacker to gain unauthorized access to, or modify, critical data. The vulnerability is difficult to exploit and is reachable via HTTPS.

Affected products:
- Hyperion Financial Data Quality Management, Enterprise Edition (11.2.26.0.000)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101848

## CVE-2026-114783

CVE-2026-114783 is an unauthenticated, remotely exploitable vulnerability in the BI Platform Security component of Oracle Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition. The vulnerability allows an attacker to compromise the application and gain unauthorized access to critical or all accessible data, with potential for cross-component impact due to a scope change.

Affected products:
- Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition (8.2.0.0.0, 12.2.1.4.0, 26.01.0.0.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114783

## CVE-2026-114792

CVE-2026-114792 is a vulnerability within the Installation component of Oracle Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition that allows an unauthenticated, local attacker to potentially achieve full system compromise. The attack vector requires the victim to perform a human action, and the attacker must have local access to the infrastructure where the software is executed.

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114792

## CVE-2026-114799

Oracle Hyperion Planning version 11.2.26.0.000 contains a security vulnerability in its Security component. An unauthenticated attacker with network access can exploit this via HTTP to gain unauthorized access to critical or full data within the application. The vulnerability has a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.5, reflecting a significant risk to data confidentiality.

Affected products:
- Hyperion Planning (11.2.26.0.000)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114799

## CVE-2026-114809

Oracle Hyperion Planning version 11.2.26.0.000 contains a security vulnerability that can be exploited by a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP. The vulnerability allows for unauthorized access to critical data and has the potential to affect other products via scope change, resulting in a high impact on confidentiality.

Affected products:
- Hyperion Planning (11.2.26.0.000)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114809

## CVE-2026-114813

A vulnerability in the Platform Security component of Oracle Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition allows an authenticated, low-privileged attacker to perform unauthorized data modification, creation, or deletion, as well as trigger a denial-of-service condition via repeatable crashes over HTTP.

Affected products:
- Oracle Business Intelligence Enterprise Edition (8.2.0.0.0, 26.01.0.0.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114813

## CVE-2026-114846

Oracle Financial Services Customer Screening version 8.1.2.10 contains a vulnerability in the User Interface component that allows an unauthenticated, network-adjacent attacker to perform unauthorized access to critical data via HTTP. The vulnerability is characterized by a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.5, primarily impacting data confidentiality.

Affected products:
- Oracle Financial Services Customer Screening (8.1.2.10)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114846

## CVE-2026-114852

Oracle REST Data Services (ORDS) contains a vulnerability in its Core component affecting versions 24.2.0 through 26.3.0. The vulnerability is network-exploitable by a low-privileged attacker via HTTP, allowing for unauthorized read, write, or deletion of critical data managed by the service. The CVSS vector (AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N) indicates high impact on confidentiality and integrity without requiring user interaction.

Affected products:
- Oracle REST Data Services (24.2.0-26.3.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114852

## CVE-2026-114860

Oracle Application Testing Suite version 13.3.0.1 is vulnerable to an unauthenticated, remote attack via TLS. The vulnerability, residing in the 'Test Manager for Web Apps' component, allows for a scope change and unauthorized access to, modification, or deletion of critical data. Due to the high confidentiality and integrity impact, defenders should monitor for anomalous network traffic directed at this component.

Affected products:
- Application Testing Suite (13.3.0.1)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114860

## CVE-2026-114865

A vulnerability in the Oracle Sales Online component of Oracle E-Business Suite (versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15) allows a low-privileged attacker with network access to compromise the application. The vulnerability requires human interaction to trigger and can result in unauthorized access to critical data and the ability to modify or delete data within the system. Due to the scope change (S:C), the impact can extend to additional integrated products.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114865

## CVE-2026-114866

Oracle E-Business Suite's Oracle Sales Online component is vulnerable to a cross-site scripting or similar attack vector that allows a low-privileged user with network access to compromise data integrity and confidentiality. The vulnerability requires user interaction and facilitates a scope change, potentially impacting other products within the suite.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114866

## CVE-2026-114867

CVE-2026-114867 is a vulnerability within the Oracle Sales Online component of Oracle E-Business Suite, affecting versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15. The vulnerability is network-exploitable via HTTP by a low-privileged attacker, potentially resulting in unauthorized modification, deletion, or creation of critical data, as well as unauthorized read access to subset data within the application.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114867

## CVE-2026-114869

CVE-2026-114869 is a vulnerability in the AD Utilities component of the Oracle E-Business Suite Applications DBA product. It allows a low-privileged user with local infrastructure access to compromise the Applications DBA component, provided that the attacker can influence or wait for human interaction from another user. Successful exploitation leads to the complete takeover of the affected component with high impact on confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

Affected products:
- E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114869

## CVE-2026-114871

Oracle Sales Online, a component of Oracle E-Business Suite versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15, contains a vulnerability in 'Internal Operations' that allows a low-privileged attacker with network access to perform unauthorized creation, deletion, or modification of critical data via HTTP. The vulnerability is rated with a CVSS 3.1 base score of 8.1, impacting confidentiality and integrity.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114871

## CVE-2026-114873

CVE-2026-114873 is a vulnerability in Oracle E-Business Suite's iProcurement component, specifically within Internal Operations. It allows a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP to compromise the application, leading to unauthorized access, modification, or deletion of critical data. The vulnerability is rated with a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.1.

Affected products:
- iProcurement (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114873

## CVE-2026-114874

CVE-2026-114874 is a vulnerability in the Internal operations component of Oracle E-Business Suite iProcurement versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15. The vulnerability allows a low-privileged attacker with network access to perform unauthorized access to critical data via HTTP. The vulnerability has a scope change (S:C) and specifically impacts confidentiality, potentially allowing an attacker to access all data reachable by the iProcurement component.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite iProcurement (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114874

## CVE-2026-114876

Oracle E-Business Suite iStore component contains a vulnerability in the Shopping Cart feature that allows an unauthenticated, network-based attacker to gain unauthorized access to critical data. Due to the high scope-changing impact and network accessibility, this vulnerability poses a significant risk to data confidentiality.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114876

## CVE-2026-114877

CVE-2026-114877 is a vulnerability in the Oracle iStore component of Oracle E-Business Suite (versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15). An unauthenticated attacker with network access via HTTP can exploit this vulnerability to perform unauthorized creation, deletion, or modification of critical iStore data, as well as gain unauthorized access to sensitive information. The vulnerability is characterized by a CVSS 3.1 base score of 7.4.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114877

## CVE-2026-114878

Oracle E-Business Suite iStore, specifically the Shopping Cart component, is vulnerable to unauthorized data access and manipulation. An attacker with low-privileged network access via HTTP can exploit this vulnerability to read, update, insert, or delete critical iStore data, resulting in significant impacts to confidentiality and integrity.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114878

Oracle Mobile Field Service within Oracle E-Business Suite versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15 contains an unauthenticated, network-exploitable vulnerability in the Admin UI component. Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114885

Oracle Mobile Field Service, a component of Oracle E-Business Suite, contains a vulnerability in the Admin UI that allows a low-privileged authenticated attacker to achieve unauthorized access to critical data. Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114888

Oracle Universal Work Queue within Oracle E-Business Suite contains a vulnerability in the Media Integration component. Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

- E-Business Suite

- Oracle E-Business Suite Secure Enterprise Search (12.2.3-12.2.15)

## CVE-2026-114916

CVE-2026-114916 is a vulnerability in the Oracle Applications Framework component of Oracle E-Business Suite, affecting versions 12.2.3 through 12.2.15. The vulnerability is exploitable by a low-privileged attacker over the network via HTTP, provided they can induce human interaction. Successful exploitation leads to a scope change and allows unauthorized access to, or modification/deletion of, critical data within the framework.

Affected products:
- Oracle E-Business Suite (12.2.3-12.2.15)

Oracle VM VirtualBox version 7.2.22 contains a vulnerability in its core component that allows an unauthenticated attacker with local logon access to perform unauthorized modifications, deletions, or data creation, as well as cause a denial of service (crash/hang). The attack requires human interaction from another user to be successful.

Affected products:
- VM VirtualBox (7.2.22)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-127423

## CVE-2026-127425

CVE-2026-127425 is a vulnerability in the Core component of Oracle VM VirtualBox version 7.2.22. It allows a high-privileged attacker with local access to the host infrastructure to compromise the application, resulting in unauthorized data access, modification, or a complete denial-of-service via application crash. Due to the scope change, successful exploitation may impact other products integrated with the virtualization environment.

Affected products:
- VM VirtualBox (7.2.22)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-127425

## CVE-2026-127426

Oracle VM VirtualBox version 7.2.22 contains a vulnerability in the Core component that allows an unauthenticated attacker on the same physical communication segment to perform unauthorized data access. The vulnerability has a scope change (S:C) and impacts confidentiality (C:H), potentially affecting other products beyond the virtualization environment.

Affected products:
- VM VirtualBox (7.2.22)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-127426

## CVE-2026-127428

CVE-2026-127428 is a vulnerability in the GIS component of Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster, allowing a low-privileged attacker with network access to perform unauthorized data reads and cause a denial-of-service (hang or crash) via multiple protocols.

Affected products:
- MySQL Server (8.4.0-8.4.13, 9.7.0-9.7.4, 26.10)

## CVE-2026-91498

An unauthenticated attacker can leverage network access via SAML to potentially update, insert, delete, or read data within Oracle Access Manager. The vulnerability has a CVSS score of 7.2 and features a scope change, indicating that successful exploitation may impact other integrated products.

- Oracle Access Manager (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.1.0)

CVE-2026-91509 is a vulnerability in the Authentication Engine of Oracle Access Manager, part of the Oracle Fusion Middleware suite. The flaw allows an unauthenticated, remote attacker to gain unauthorized access to or modify critical data within Oracle Access Manager via specifically crafted SAML requests. Due to the scope change associated with this vulnerability, successful exploitation can have broad security implications for integrated systems managed by the Authentication Engine.

Affected products:
- Oracle Access Manager (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.1.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-91509

CVE-2026-114928 is a critical vulnerability in the Oracle PeopleSoft Enterprise PeopleTools Application Server (versions 8.61-8.63). The vulnerability allows a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP to fully compromise the PeopleSoft instance, resulting in a complete takeover of confidentiality, integrity, and availability. The flaw is rated as easily exploitable due to the low complexity of the attack vector.

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114928

## CVE-2026-114929

A vulnerability in the Application Server component of Oracle PeopleSoft Enterprise PeopleTools versions 8.61 through 8.63 allows a low-privileged attacker with network access via HTTP to potentially take over the affected application. The vulnerability carries a CVSS 3.1 score of 7.5, reflecting significant impacts to confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

Affected products:
- PeopleSoft Enterprise PeopleTools (8.61-8.63)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-114929

## CVE-2026-114949

CVE-2026-114949 is a vulnerability in the Server: Replication component of Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster. The vulnerability allows a high-privileged attacker with network access via multiple protocols to fully compromise the MySQL Server or MySQL Cluster instance, leading to impacts on confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

- MySQL Server (8.4.0-8.4.13, 9.7.0-9.7.4, 26.10)
- MySQL Cluster (8.0.0-8.0.50, 8.4.0-8.4.13, 9.7.0-9.7.4, 26.10)

- Oracle WebCenter Portal (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.0.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-91586

## CVE-2026-91587

CVE-2026-91587 is a critical vulnerability in Oracle WebCenter Portal, specifically within the Composer component. The vulnerability allows an unauthenticated, network-adjacent attacker to perform unauthorized actions via HTTP requests, resulting in potential exposure of critical data. Due to the scope change (S:C), successful exploitation can lead to impact beyond the initial component. The vulnerability is characterized by a high confidentiality impact (CVSS 3.1 Base Score 8.6).

Affected products:
- WebCenter Portal (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.0.0)

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-91587

The vulnerability includes a scope change, meaning successful exploitation can negatively impact other connected products within the environment.

- Oracle Access Manager (12.2.1.4.0, 14.1.2.1.0)

Oracle Communications Unified Inventory Management contains a vulnerability in its security component that allows an unauthenticated, network-adjacent attacker to perform unauthorized access to critical data via HTTP. The vulnerability is characterized by a high confidentiality impact (CVSS 7.5), as attackers can potentially access all data within the affected system without requiring authentication.

A vulnerability in the JSON component of Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster allows a low-privileged, network-authenticated attacker to perform unauthorized data modification, deletion, or creation, as well as unauthorized data reading and denial-of-service via server hangs or crashes. The vulnerability is characterized by a high CVSS score due to its impacts on confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

CVE-2026-101786 is a high-severity vulnerability in the Optimizer component of Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster versions 26.7.0 through 26.7.1. An attacker with high privileges and network access can exploit this flaw via multiple protocols to achieve a full system compromise (takeover) of the affected MySQL services.

- MySQL Cluster (26.7.0-26.7.1)

## CVE-2026-101796

Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster contain a vulnerability in the Replication component that allows an unauthenticated, remote attacker to gain full control of the database server. Exploitation is categorized as difficult and requires human interaction, such as a victim performing an action, to succeed over network protocols.

Source: https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-101796

## CVE-2026-101797

CVE-2026-101797 is a high-severity vulnerability in the mysqldump client component of Oracle MySQL Server and MySQL Cluster. It allows an unauthenticated, network-adjacent attacker to achieve full compromise (confidentiality, integrity, and availability) of the affected database systems. Successful exploitation is difficult and requires human interaction, such as a user running the mysqldump utility in a compromised environment.

- MySQL Server (8.4.0-8.4.12, 9.7.0-9.7.3, 26.7.0-26.7.1)
